Minimal XML escaping for element text nodes.\n    return (\n        text.replace(\"&\", \"&amp;\")\n        .replace(\"<\", \"&lt;\")\n        .replace(\">\", \"&gt;\")\n        .replace('\"', \"&quot;\")\n        .replace(\"'\", \"&apos;\")\n    )\n\n\ndef _normalize_budget(raw: str) -> str:\n    s = (raw or \"\").strip()\n    m = re.match(r\"^([^0-9]+)([0-9,]+)$\", s)\n    if m:\n        return m.group(1) + m.group(2).replace(\",\", \"\")\n    return s.replace(\",\", \"\")\n\n\ndef _month_index(year: int, month: int) -> int:\n    return year * 12 + (month - 1)\n\n\n_RANGE_RE = re.compile(\n    r\"(?P<sY>20\\d{2})[.\\-/](?P<sM>\\d{1,2})(?:[.\\-/]\\d{1,2})?\\s*~\\s*\"\n    r\"(?P<eY>20\\d{2})[.\\-/](?P<eM>\\d{1,2})(?:[.\\-/]\\d{1,2})?\"\n)\n_SINGLE_RE = re.compile(r\"(20\\d{2})[.\\-/](\\d{1,2})(?:[.\\-/](\\d{1,2}))?\")\n\n\ndef _calc_month_span(text: str) -> int | None:\n    \"\"\"Return inclusive month span based on the first date/range found.\"\"\"\n    m = _RANGE_RE.search(text)\n    if m:\n        sY, sM = int(m.group(\"sY\")), int(m.group(\"sM\"))\n        eY, eM = int(m.group(\"eY\")), int(m.group(\"eM\"))\n        if not (1 <= sM <= 12 and 1 <= eM <= 12):\n            return None\n        span = _month_index(eY, eM) - _month_index(sY, sM) + 1\n        return span if span > 0 else None\n\n    m2 = _SINGLE_RE.search(text)\n    if m2:\n        # Single month/date reference ⇒ treat as 1 month.\n        return 1\n\n    return None\n\n\n_PLACEHOLDER_RE = re.compile(r\"\\{\\{([^{}]+)\\}\\}\")\n\n\ndef _replace_placeholders(plain_text: str, mapping: dict[str, str]) -> tuple[str, bool]:\n    modified = False\n\n    def _sub(m: re.Match) -> str:\n        nonlocal modified\n        key = m.group(1)\n        if key not in mapping:\n            raise KeyError(f\"No JSON value provided for placeholder key: {key!r}\")\n        modified = True\n        return mapping[key]\n\n    out = _PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(_sub, plain_text)\n    return out, modified\n\n\n_T_RE = re.compile(r\"<hp:t\\b[^>]*>(.*?)</hp:t>\", re.DOTALL)\n_P_RE = re.compile(r\"(<hp:p\\b[^>]*>)(.*?)(</hp:p>)\", re.DOTALL)\n\n\ndef _get_para_id(open_tag: str) -> str | None:\n    m = re.search(r'\\bid=\"([^\"]+)\"', open_tag)\n    return m.group(1) if m else None\n\n\ndef _detect_phase_num(text: str) -> int | None:\n    # Avoid double-appending.\n    if re.search(r\"\\(\\s*\\d+\\s*개월\\s*\\)\", text):\n        return text, False\n\n    span = _calc_month_span(text)\n    if span is None:\n        # If the paragraph is a phase line but we can't parse the span, leave it unchanged.\n        return text, False\n\n    return text.rstrip() + f\" ({span}개월)\", True\n\n\n_LINESEG_RE = re.compile(\n    r\"<hp:(?:lineSegArray|linesegarray)\\b[^>]*/\\s*>|\"\n    r\"<hp:(?:lineSegArray|linesegarray)\\b[^>]*>.*?</hp:(?:lineSegArray|linesegarray)>\",\n    re.DOTALL,\n)\n\n\ndef _rebuild_modified_paragraph(open_p: str, original_inner: str, close_p: str, new_text: str) -> str:\n    # Preserve the first run's charPrIDRef if present; otherwise default to 0.\n    m = re.search(r'<hp:run\\b[^>]*\\bcharPrIDRef=\"([^\"]+)\"', original_inner)\n    char_pr = m.group(1) if m else \"0\"\n\n    #
